- **feature-scope**: A feature's scope is its `apps` list. Adding an app to the workspace and retiring one never change a feature's scope by themselves; this skill is the explicit edit. Before `ready-for-dev` it is an ordinary write: at `ready-for-design` or `in-design` it also sets the owner to the design owner of the new scope (`designer` while an active app has a UI, otherwise `tech-lead`). From `ready-for-dev` on ([mo0rti/prism](https://github.com/mo0rti/prism/blob/HEAD/golden/.claude/commands/feature-scope.md) · ★1)
